| `bun run check` (`oxfmt --check` → `oxlint --type-aware` → `tsc --noEmit` + per-workspace `tsc` → `check-invariants.ts`) | 0 | 6.21s wall |
| `bun run build` (all 5 workspaces) | 0 | 1.87s wall |

`check` emitted only pre-existing `effecttsgo` warnings in `packages/boot/test/fixtures/*` and `packages/server/pages/tooling/evlog-sink.ts` (none in this PR's new files). `check:invariants` printed `Workspace import boundaries passed.` despite `@comms/storage` gaining `@effect/sql-pg` and `@effect/sql-mysql2`.

# B. Test runs (4 invocations, `npx vitest run … --reporter=verbose`)

**Group 1 — every test file this PR adds or changes under `packages/storage`** (`dialect`, `remote-session`, `remote-sessions`, `remote-values`, `mysql-pool-cleanup`, `store`): **5 files passed | 1 skipped; 19 tests passed | 7 skipped; 5.90s.** The skipped file is `remote-sessions.test.ts` — all 7 of its cases.

**Group 2 — descriptor / store / message / mention / read-mark** (`store-descriptor-compatibility`, `message-filters`, `message-http`, `message-wait-cursor`, `mention-read-marks`): **5 files, 7 tests passed, 7.56s.** The two base-PR semantics fixes are covered and green here: `mention-read-marks.test.ts > recognizes punctuation-terminated mentions without shortening another valid target` (3ms) and `> marks only the requested subtree in OR results and never globally marks a mentions-only view` (1071ms).

**Group 3 — topic / page / public-page** (`topics-http`, `topic-management-http`, `topic-move-http`, `topic-move-drain`, `boot/topic-move-recovery`, `server/pages-http`, `boot/pages-http`, `page-failure`, `page-publication-queue`, `page-revert`, `public-page-policy`, `public-page-migration`, `public-page-reload`, `server/public-pages`, `boot/public-pages`): **15 files, 52 tests passed, 38.08s.**

**Group 4 — the two non-storage test files this PR changes** (`boot/test/request-events.test.ts` +13, `server/test/generation-revert.test.ts` +12): **2 files, 13 tests passed, 139.19s** (slowest single case `restores a retained generation's whole source and manifest through cutover without restoring messages`, 63.7s).

**Local total: 91 passed, 7 skipped, 0 failed.** No failure to re-run, so no flaky/deterministic classification was needed from my own runs — but see finding 1, which came from CI at this exact head and which I did re-run three times.

# Findings

### 1. major — the Linux workflow is **red at the reviewed head** `686e07c`, and the body does not say so

`gh run list --branch codex/database-portability-resumed` for `name == "Linux"`:

```
34680113468 success 74f8d03
34679210722 failure 686e07c   <- reviewed head
34677327427 failure cc00444
34677010732 cancelled 1ba09dc
34675617283 success 83e312d
```

Run 34679210722, job `verify (2)`, step *Boot, server and storage recovery tests*:

```
× packages/server/test/sql-write-recovery.test.ts > retires the child after an implicit
  SQLite constraint rollback before resuming writes  4995ms
AssertionError: expected [ Array(1) ] to deeply equal [ { pending_id: null } ]
Test Files  1 failed | 101 passed | 1 skipped (103)
     Tests  1 failed | 410 passed | 1 skipped (412)
```

The preceding commit `cc00444` failed Linux on two *different* tests (`generation-revert.test.ts > restores a retained generation's whole source…`, `Caused by: AssertionError: expected 'starting' to be 'live'`, and `request-events.test.ts > fences stored request publication…`, `expected { _tag: 'Failure' … } to match object { _tag: 'Success' }`). I ran `sql-write-recovery.test.ts` locally three times: **3/3 passed** (3116ms/3257ms, 2921ms/2863ms, 2907ms/2800ms), so it is not deterministic on darwin — the failures are timing-sensitive under the 2-shard/2-worker CI load. But three consecutive CI runs on this branch with three different red tests is a cost, not a non-event: the body's "All nine failures from the preceding run are corrected" is stated without the qualifier that new ones appeared twice afterwards. The body's hedge "exact newer-head results remain separate" is true but reads as *untested*, not as *tested and failing*.

**Also: the PR head has already moved past the commit under review.** `gh pr view 8 --json headRefOid` returns `74f8d034e2…`, whose single parent is `686e07c` and whose only change is `packages/server/test/sql-write-recovery.test.ts +42 -1` — it scopes the over-broad `SELECT pending_id FROM seq` assertion to the retired writer's own reservation and adds a new, sharper test (`distinguishes an aborted old reservation from legitimate replacement publication`). That is a legitimate fix to an over-strong assertion rather than a weakening, and Linux is green at `74f8d03`. Reviewers should be told the PR is one commit ahead of `686e07c`.

### 2. note — all three named validation claims check out exactly; the skip counts are complementary, not hidden failures

All three cited commits are ancestors of `686e07c` (`git merge-base --is-ancestor`: `de617e7` ✓, `1ba09dc` ✓, `83e312d` ✓).

**The 848/8/204/2 claim is internally consistent.** `find packages -path '*/test/*' -name '*.test.ts'` counts exactly **206** files = 204 + 2. The only three files with `skipIf` are `packages/storage/test/remote-sessions.test.ts` (7 cases, all skip without `COMMS_REMOTE_TEST_CONFIG`), `packages/server/test/cutover-acceptance.test.ts:20` (1 case, `skipIf(process.env.COMMS_CUTOVER_ACCEPTANCE !== "1")`), and `packages/boot/test/storage-volume.test.ts:144` (`skipIf(platform !== "linux" && platform !== "darwin")` — runs on both CI and my machine). 7 + 1 = **8 skipped across exactly 2 fully-skipped files.** `de617e7..HEAD` touches only `docs/*`, `request-events.test.ts`, the remote fixtures and the acceptance script, so the 848 figure is not stale in any load-bearing way.

**The remote-session CI claim is exact.** `gh run view 34677010761` → `headSha: 1ba09dc5…`, `conclusion: success`, three jobs. Per-job logs:

| Leg | Claimed | Observed in log |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `sessions (pg, 1024)` | 6 passed 1 skipped | `6 passed \| 1 skipped (7)` — leases/reject/pending/stream/restart/prepared ✓, `missing` ↓ |
| `sessions (mysql, 1024)` | 5 passed 2 skipped | `5 passed \| 2 skipped (7)` — `prepared` and `missing` ↓ |
| `sessions (mysql, 32)` | 1 passed 6 skipped | `1 passed \| 6 skipped (7)` — only `missing` ✓ |

The "six of seven skip" leg is not a degenerate pass. `remote-sessions.test.ts:9-13` gates on `(mode === "missing") !== (COMMS_REMOTE_TEST_ATTRIBUTES === "32")`, and `.github/workflows/remote-sessions.yml:22-24` runs a third leg with `attributes: 32`, which `scripts/remote-session-acceptance.sh:56` passes as `--performance-schema-session-connect-attrs-size=32`. That leg exists *solely* to prove the inspector refuses to start when MySQL truncates the `comms_attempt` connect attribute it depends on for session identity (`fixtures/remote-sessions.ts:59-71` asserts `configured == "32"` then asserts `Layer.build(remoteInspectorLayer(...))` fails). The union of the three legs covers all 7 modes against real servers, and the logs confirm real engines were pulled (`postgres:17.11-bookworm@sha256:051f…`, `mysql:8.4.11@sha256:3466…`).

**One small inaccuracy:** the body says "Native PostgreSQL 18.6/MySQL 8.4.11 client probes". The CI acceptance pins **PostgreSQL 17.11** (`scripts/remote-session-acceptance.sh:8`). 18.6 can only be the author's separate local probe; the reproducible evidence is 17.11. Worth correcting in the body so nobody assumes 18.x is covered.

### 3. major — no remote **engine** is exercised by the default test suite, and the dialect SQL that is the point of the track is never executed against PostgreSQL or MySQL anywhere

`bun run test` (and therefore the Linux workflow, which runs `bun run test packages/boot/test packages/server/test packages/storage/test`) sets no `COMMS_REMOTE_TEST_CONFIG`, so all 7 real-server cases skip — confirmed in my Group 1 run (`↓ real remote session leases` … `↓ real remote session missing`). Only the separate `remote-sessions.yml` workflow supplies it. That is a defensible split, but two specific gaps follow that the body's "Core message/topic queries use those fragments while preserving SQLite behavior" does not disclose:

- **`packages/storage/test/dialect.test.ts` is string-comparison only for the remote engines.** `dialect.test.ts:22` builds the constructor as `Statement.make(Effect.die("Compile only"), compiler(name), [], undefined)`, and the assertions are `expect(text).toContain("starts_with(\"topic\"::text,$1::text||'/')")` etc. Only the SQLite branch is *run* — via `new DatabaseSync(...)` (`dialect.test.ts:1`). So `isDescendant`'s `BINARY substr(…)=BINARY CONCAT(…)`, `jsonText`'s MySQL `JSON_TYPE(...)='NULL'` case, `jsonArrayHas`'s `jsonb_array_elements_text`, `distinctFrom`'s `NOT (a <=> b)` and `jsonInt`'s `CAST(… AS SIGNED)` (`dialect.ts:22-85`) have **never been executed by any engine**. A typo inside a pg/mysql template string that still matches the `toContain` substring, or a semantic mistake like MySQL `CAST('abc' AS SIGNED)` returning `0` where Postgres `::bigint` errors, passes every check in this repo.
- **The real-server fixture never touches the dialect module or the rewritten core queries.** `packages/storage/test/fixtures/remote-sessions.ts` imports only `remote-client.ts`, `remote-inspector.ts`, `remote-session.ts`, `remote-driver.ts`; its SQL is `CREATE TABLE remote_probe_<mode>(value INTEGER)`, `INSERT … VALUES(1..4)`, `SELECT value …`, plus the `9007199254740991`/`…92` BIGINT sentinels (`:181-196`). So the PostgreSQL/MySQL CI proves the *connection lease, identity, sanitization and integer guard* work against real servers — which it genuinely does, well — and proves nothing about the twelve rewritten query modules. The two things the owner cares about ("swapping … and everything should swap work") and that the base PR just fixed (mention grammar, read-mark scoping) are SQLite-verified only.

This is the honest reading of the body's own last paragraph ("does not … complete swappable database support"), so it is a status for the *board*; but "Core message/topic queries use those fragments while preserving SQLite behavior" asserts preservation that is only demonstrated on SQLite, and the dialect-fragment-vs-real-engine gap is not named anywhere in the body. The cheap fix is to parameterize `dialect.test.ts` to execute its pg and mysql branches inside the existing `remote-sessions.yml` legs, where live servers already exist.

### 4. note — the runtime-staging claim is verified

"Runtime staging carries the exact driver dependencies and MySQL pool-cleanup patch" holds. `packages/storage/package.json` adds `@effect/sql-mysql2` and `@effect/sql-pg` at `4.0.0-rc.113`; `packages/server/runtime/package.json` lists `storage` as a workspace and adds the `patchedDependencies` entry; `packages/server/runtime/bun.lock:44-45,52,117-119,489` resolves both plus `mysql2@3.24.4` and records the patch; and `packages/server/stage-runtime.ts:21-26` copies `patches/@effect%2Fsql-mysql2@4.0.0-rc.113.patch` into the staged tree with the comment "The standalone installer must apply the same remote pool-ownership fix." The patch itself moves pool creation inside `Effect.acquireRelease` so a failed or timed-out `SELECT 1` handshake still ends the pool — and `packages/storage/test/mysql-pool-cleanup.test.ts` exercises that against the real copied `@effect/sql-mysql2` in a throwaway `node_modules` (both cases passed, 161ms and 5207ms, the latter being the 5s handshake timeout). This is one of the few places in the stack where a real driver is tested in the default suite.
